```
def _corr(a,s,n):
    L=2*n-1
    N=next_fast_len(3*n-2)
    A=rfft(a,N)
    S=rfft(s[::-1],N)
    c=irfft(A*S,N)[:3*n-2]
    return c[L-1:L-n-1:-1] ?
```

Detailed code structure:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len

```
Pre-import evaluate_sequence. height_sequence_1 available.

Helper `_clip_seq(a)`, `_fft_conv_sq(a)`:
```
def conv_self(a):
    n=a.size
    L=2*n-1
    N=next_fast_len(L)
    F=rfft(a,N)
    c=irfft(F*F,N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c,0, out=c)
    # maybe normalize tiny negative
    return c
```
For exact score:
```
def exact_score(a):
    n=a.size; S=a.sum()
    if S<0.01: return inf
    # score scale-invariant; normalize for speed
    an=a/S
    D=conv_self(an)
    return 2*n*D.max()
```
But for evaluating many internal candidates, normalized already. Use `exact_score_norm(a, n): return 2*n*conv_self(a).max()` assuming sum=1. To avoid repeated conv, in optimizers compute D once.

For final `evaluate_sequence(input_list)`.

Log optimizer class:
```
class LogLSE:
    def __init__(self, a, n, T=50.0, ref=None, bound=50.0):
        self.n=n
        a=np.clip(a,0,1000).copy(); a[a<=0]=1e-300? 
        if a.sum()<=0: a=np.ones(n)/n
        a=a/a.sum()
        # ref: index with max
        self.ref = int(np.argmax(a)) if ref is None else ref
        if a[self.ref] < 1e-300: a[self.ref]=1e-300; a/=a.sum()
        mask=np.ones(n,bool); mask[self.ref]=False
        self.idx=np.nonzero(mask)[0]
        y=np.log(a[self.idx]/a[self.ref]); y=np.clip(y,-bound,bound)
        self.y0=y.copy()
        self.best_a=a.copy(); self.best_score=exact_score_norm(a)
        self.T=T
        # precompute N_conv=next_fast_len(2n-1), N_corr=next_fast_len(3n-2)
    def _a_from_y(self,y):
       z=np.zeros(n); z[self.ref]=0; z[self.idx]=y; z-=z.max(); e=np.exp(z); a=e/e.sum(); return a
    def _objgrad(self,y, exact_track=False):
       z=...; a=...
       D=conv_self(a)
       # exact score track
       mD=D.max(); score=2*n*mD
       # logs
       Dsafe=np.where(D>0, D, 1e-300)
       L=np.log(Dsafe)
       m=L.max()
       u=L-m
       # stability
       T=self.T
       # max u maybe 0; min very negative; if T*min < -745 exp underflow.
       ex=np.exp(np.clip(T*u, -745, 0)) # since u<=0
       sume=ex.sum()
       if sume<=0: ...
       H=m + math.log(sume)/T
       alpha=ex/sume
       s=np.zeros(n? L length)
       pos=Dsafe>1e-300? alpha>0
       s[pos]=alpha[pos]/Dsafe[pos]
       C=corr(a,s,n)
       g=2*a*(C-1)
       # grad_y = g[idx]
       # Optionally recenter gradient: Since parameter y last? For shift-invariant, g sum zero. But if numerical, we can subtract weighted? Let's derive derivative of F(y) with respect to y when z has ref fixed. F(z) shift-invariant. If g is gradient wrt z, g_y = g[idx]. Good.
       # To improve, enforce g_y sum? Not needed.
       return H, g[idx].copy()
```

```
def interpolate_to(a, n_new, method='linear'):
    n_old=len(a); S=a.sum(); a=a/S
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n_old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
    f_old=a*n_old
    f_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, f_old)
    # optional smooth filter? 
    a_new=np.maximum(f_new,0)/n_new
    a_new/=a_new.sum()
    return a_new
```

```
    optimizer = _LogLSE(a_init,n,T=T_vals[0])
    y = optimizer.y0.copy()

                bounds=[(-bound,bound)]*optimizer.y0.size,

                callback=cb)
```

Vectorized evaluation function for coordinate:
```
def eval_x_grid(x_grid, D, infl, two_i, v):
    w = x_grid - v
    # D_new = D[None,:] + w[:,None]*infl[None,:]
    # For 2i add w^2
    if two_i>=0:
       temp = D + w*infl[?]
       temp[:,two_i] += w*w
    denom = (1+w)**2
    val = temp.max(axis=1)/denom
    return val*2n
```

```
def _get_parent():
  try: h=globals().get('height_sequence_1')
  except: h=None
  if h is None: h=np.ones(384)
  p=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).ravel()
  p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0); p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
  if p.size==0 or p.sum()<0.01: p=np.ones(384)
  return p
```

Internal score:
```
def _score_norm(a): # assume sum ~1
    n=a.size
    D=_conv_self(a,n)
    M=D.max()
    return 2.0*n*M
def _score_any(a):
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-300: return inf
    a=a/S
    return _score_norm(a)
```

```
  y=opt.y0.copy(); best_a=a0; best_sc=opt.best_score
  # Run T seq
  for T in T_seq:
      opt.T=T
      if time>stop: break
      opts={... 'maxfun': maybe 3000}
      cb = lambda x: time.monotonic()>stop_time-15
      try: res=minimize(obj,y,...)
      except: pass
      y=res.x if ok
      a=opt.a_from_y(y); sc=score; update
  # restarts with smaller T (80) while time left

     sigma = base_sigma * (1 + 0.5*restarts) maybe anneal

     run short minimize maxfun=500
     restarts++
  # final T=1000 short
  return best_a,best_sc,y
```

Now, let's detail `_interpolate_density`:
```
def _interp(a, n_new):
    if len(a)==n_new: return a.copy()
    a=np.asarray(a,float); S=a.sum()
    if S<=0: return np.ones(n_new)/n_new
    a=a/S
    n=len(a)
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    f_old=a*n
    f_new=np.interp(x_new,x_old,f_old)
    f_new=np.maximum(f_new,0.0)
    # if f_new sum zero fallback
    out=f_new/n_new
    s=out.sum()
    if s<0.01: out=np.ones(n_new)/n_new
    else: out/=s
    return out
```
Could use `np.interp` with endpoints; if parent has endpoint zeros, okay. Maybe to preserve integral better, use cumulative trapezoidal? Linear fine.

Maybe generate random Dirichlet inits for small n:
```
def _random_dirichlet(n, alpha, rng): return rng.dirichlet(np.full(n,alpha)); normalize.
```
Use for restarts/alternative lengths. But parent better. For high n, random may high score but L-BFGS could find basin? Maybe not.

Need include "genuinely different compactly generated initializations" guided by strategy: GP/annealing not necessary. We can create simple param shapes:
- linear ramp up/down: `a = 1 + beta*x`
- skew power: `a = (1+beta*x)^p`
- inverted: `1 - beta*x`
- exponential: `exp(beta*x)`

Imports:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
```

Exception:

```
class _TimeUp(Exception): pass
```

Conv:
```
def _conv_self(a, N=None, L=None):
    n=a.size
    if n==1:
       return np.array([float(a[0]*a[0])])
    if L is None: L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=next_fast_len(L)
    F=rfft(a,N)
    c=irfft(F*F,N)[:L]
    if c.size:
       np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```
Using `out=c` on slice? c is new array. Good.

Score:
```
def _score_norm(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   d=a.sum()
   if d<=1e-300: return math.inf
   if abs(d-1)>1e-10: a=a/d
   D=_conv_self(a)
   M=float(D.max()) if D.size else 0
   if not math.isfinite(M) or M<0: return math.inf
   return 2.0*n*M
```
For speed in loops, if already normalized skip sum? We can have `_assumed_norm_score(a): D...`.

Maybe parent heights can be >1 normalized? `a=a/sum` always.

LogLSE:
```
class _LogLSE:
    def __init__(self, a, T=40.0, bound=70.0):
       self.n=int(a.size)
       a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
       a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
       s=a.sum()
       if s<0.01? if <1e-300: a=np.ones(self.n)/self.n; s=1
       a=a/s
       # add tiny floor to all? If some zero and reference not zero, log zero issue.
       # We will set zeros to exp(-bound) relative to ref maybe.
       # choose ref with max
       self.ref=int(np.argmax(a))
       if a[self.ref] < 1e-300:
           a[self.ref]=1e-300; a/=a.sum()
       # floor relative? y log(a[i]/a_ref). If a[i]=0, log 0 => -inf. Set y to floor.
       min_ratio=math.exp(-bound) # e-70
       # y = clip(log(a/a_ref), -bound, bound), with zeros -> -bound
       y = np.full(self.n, -bound)
       pos = a > a[self.ref]*min_ratio*0.5? Actually ratio.
       mask = a > 0
       y[mask] = np.log(a[mask]/a[self.ref])
       y = np.clip(y, -bound, bound)
       self.idx = np.array([i for i in range(self.n) if i != self.ref], dtype=int)
       self.y0 = y[self.idx].copy()
       self.bound=bound
       self.T=T
       self.L=2*n-1
       self.N_conv=next_fast_len(self.L)
       self.N_corr=next_fast_len(3*n-2 if n>1 else 1)
       self.best_a=a.copy()
       self.best_score=_score_norm(self.best_a)
       self.best_y=self.y0.copy()
       self._call_count=0
    def _a(self,y):
       z=np.zeros(self.n,float)
       z[self.ref]=0.0
       if y.size: z[self.idx]=y
       zmax=z.max()
       e=np.exp(z-zmax)
       s=e.sum()
       if s<=0 or not finite: fallback
       return e/s
    def _corr(self,a,s):
       n=self.n; L=self.L
       if n==1: return np.array([sum(a*s)])? For n=1, C0=a0*s0.
       N=self.N_corr
       r=s[::-1]
       A=rfft(a,N); B=rfft(r,N)
       c=irfft(A*B,N)[:3*n-2]
       # slice
       C=c[2*n-2 : n-1 : -1]
       if C.size != n: C = np.zeros(n)
       C=np.ascontiguousarray(C)
       np.maximum(C,0.0,out=C)
       return C
    def objgrad(self,y, deadline=None):
       y=np.asarray(y,float)
       if deadline is not None and time.monotonic()>deadline:
           raise _TimeUp()
       n=self.n
       a=self._a(y)
       D=_conv_self_with_N(a, self.N_conv, self.L) # inline
       Mmax=float(D.max()) if D.size else 0
       score=2.0*n*Mmax if math.isfinite(Mmax) else math.inf
       self._call_count +=1
       if math.isfinite(score) and score < self.best_score - 1e-14:
           self.best_score=score
           self.best_a=a.copy()
           self.best_y=y.copy()
       # if score inf, return inf
       Dsafe=np.where(D>1e-300, D, 1e-300)
       L=np.log(Dsafe)
       m=L.max()
       T=self.T
       if T <= 0:
           # subgradient uniform max
           active = L > m - 1e-12
           if active.sum()==0: active=np.empty... 
       else:
           Tu = T*(L-m)
           Tu = np.where(Tu < -745.0, -745.0, Tu)
           e = np.exp(Tu)
           sume = e.sum()
           if not finite or sume<=0:
              return (1e300, np.zeros(y.size))
           H = float(m + math.log(sume)/T)
           alpha = e/sume
       s = np.zeros(self.L)
       # If T<=0: need values. For T>0 alpha/D
       np.divide(alpha, Dsafe, out=s, where=Dsafe>1e-300)
       C = self._corr(a,s)
       g = 2.0*a*(C - 1.0)
       # if not finite
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
           return (1e300, np.zeros(y.size))
       # optional damp if huge
       gmax=np.max(np.abs(g))
       if gmax>1e6: g *= 1e6/gmax
       gy=g[self.idx]
       return (float(H), gy)
```

`minimize` call:
```
def _run_lsq(y0, T, stop_time, maxfun, gtol):
   opt.T=T
   deadline = stop_time - 15
   def fun(y):
       return opt.objgrad(y, deadline)
   def cb(x):
       return time.monotonic() > stop_time - 25
   bounds=[(-opt.bound,opt.bound) for _ in range(opt.y0.size)]
   try:
      res=minimize(fun, y0, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
        options={'maxiter': maxfun//5?,'maxfun':maxfun,'gtol':gtol,'ftol':1e-14,'disp':False}, callback=cb)
      if res.x is not None:
         y=res.x
         # evaluate final exact
         a=opt._a(y); sc=_score_norm(a); update opt best
         return y
   except _TimeUp: pass
   except Exception: pass
   return y0
```
But `minimize` options `maxiter` not necessarily; if maxfun large, maxiter default? Set `maxiter=min(maxfun, 20000)`. If objective raises _TimeUp, we catch. However if `minimize` fails due to line search, returns res status not 0 but x maybe; still use.

`_track_opt` can include restarts:
```
def _log_optimize(a_init, n, stop_time, seed=0, T_levels=(10,40,120), restarts=0, maxfun_per=None):
    opt=_LogLSE(a_init,T=T_levels[0])
    y=opt.y0.copy()
    base_maxfun = int(max(800, min(8000, 4000000/max(1,n)))) # for n=384 8000, 1024 3906, 2048 1953
    # T levels
    for T in T_levels:
       if time.monotonic()>stop_time-30: break
       maxfun = base_maxfun
       # if time left small, reduce
       remain=stop_time-time.monotonic()
       if remain<20: break
       y=_run_lsq(y,T,min(stop_time, time.monotonic()+min(remain, 120?)), maxfun, gtol=1e-9)
    # restarts
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+1)
    # use best_y
    for r in range(restarts):
       if time.monotonic()>stop_time-40: break
       sigma = 0.12 * (r+1) maybe start 0.1, up to 1
       ytry = opt.best_y + rng.normal(0,sigma,size=opt.y0.size)
       ytry = np.clip(ytry, -opt.bound, opt.bound)
       ytry=_run_lsq(ytry, 60.0, min(stop_time, time.monotonic()+40), max(500, base_maxfun//3), 1e-8)
    # final fine T=200 short
    if time<stop_time-30:
       y=_run_lsq(y, 200.0, ..., maxfun//4)
    return opt.best_a.copy(), opt.best_score
```
Allocation of time per run: If stop_time is end for this length, _run_lsq uses stop_time-15 and callback stops. Multiple runs may run until stop. But if T levels each consume until stop? In code, if `_run_lsq` called with stop_time=end, it may exhaust all time for first level. Need allocate slices. Better pass `run_stop` for each T: `run_stop = min(stop_time, time.monotonic()+alloc)`.

Implementation of LP `_lp_polish(a, stop)`:
Need not too slow. Use internal score.
```
def _lp_polish(a, stop_time, max_rounds=4):
   n=a.size
   if n>512: return a, _score_norm(a)
   a=a.copy()/sum
   best_sc=_score_norm(a); best=a.copy()
   D=_conv_self(a); M=D.max()
   for r in range(max_rounds):
      if time>stop_time-10: break
      A=np.zeros((2*n-1,n))
      for i in range(n): A[i:i+n,i]=a[i]
      c=-np.ones(n)
      b_ub=np.full(2*n-1,M)
      try:
         res=linprog(c, A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=[(0,1.0)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit': min(10, stop_time-now-8),'presolve':True})
      except: break
      if res.status not in (0,?) or res.x is None: break
      g=res.x.astype(float); gsum=g.sum()
      if gsum<1e-9: break
      g=g/gsum
      # line search t
      best_t=0; best_local=best_sc
      ts=np.concatenate([np.linspace(0.02,0.8,20), np.array([1.0])])? t=1 replace with g; since sum1.
      for t in ts:
         cnew=(1-t)*best + t*g
         cnew=np.maximum(cnew,0); cnew/=cnew.sum()
         sc=_score_norm(cnew)
         if sc<best_local: best_local=sc; best_t=t
      if best_t>0 and best_local<best_sc-1e-12:
         best=...; best_sc=best_local; D=_conv_self(best); M=D.max()
      else: break
   return best, best_sc
```
But LP constraint uses M of `a`, not `best` updated? Should use current best D. For first round, current. Good.
`linprog` bounds (0,1) may restrict if direction needs >1, but normalized sum1, okay. If a has zeros causing unbounded, bounds help. Time limit. If HiGHS in environment? yes.

Potential issue: Building A as columns `a[i]` rows i:i+n. For convex a*b: row k, col i coefficient = a_i if 0<=k-i<n. For i=0, rows0..n-1 a0. For i=1 rows1..n a1. Correct.

Coordinate polish `_coord_polish` as described. Need use time. For n<=768, grid ~100. Let's implement carefully.

```
def _coord_polish(a, stop_time, max_sweeps=4, seed=1):
    n=a.size
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()/a.sum()
    best_sc=_score_norm(a); best=a.copy()
    D=_conv_self(a)
    nge=90
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
       if time.monotonic()>stop_time-20: break
       improved=False
       order=rng.permutation(n)
       for i in order:
          if time.monotonic()>stop_time-25: break
          v=best[i]
          L=2*n-1
          infl=np.zeros(L)
          start=i; end=min(i+n,L); m=end-start
          if m>0: infl[start:end]=2.0*best[:m]
          # grid
          if v > 0:
             xs = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,60)
             # add near v
             lo=max(0.0,0.1*v); hi=min(1.0,10.0*v); if hi<=lo: hi=min(1,v*2+1e-3)
             xs=np.concatenate([xs, np.linspace(lo,hi,20), [v, min(1,max(0, v*0.5)), min(1,v*2)]])
          else:
             xs=np.spawn(0.0,1.0,60) plus log space 1e-8..0.1
          xs=np.unique(np.clip(xs,0,1))
          # if xs.size >150: take subset
          if xs.size>120: xs=xs[::xs.size//120+1]
          if 0 not in xs: xs=np.sort(np.concatenate([[0],xs]))
          S=1.0
          # eval
          w=xs-v
          # skip w where 1+w <=1e-12
          keep = (1+w)>1e-8
          if keep.sum()==0: continue
          xx=xs[keep]; ww=w[keep]
          # num = D + ww*infl
          num = D[None,:] + ww[:,None]*infl[None,:]
          if 0 <= 2*i < L: num[:,2*i] += ww*ww
          # max over k, but num may negative
          # use np.maximum(num,0, out=num)
          np.maximum(num,0.0,out=num)
          val = np.max(num, axis=1) / ((1+ww)**2)
          val *= 2*n
          # refine
          j0=int(np.argmin(val)); xbest=xx[j0]; scbest=float(val[j0])
          # refine grid if xbest not at 0/1
          span = (xx.max()-xx.min())/ (xs.size-1 +1)
          xlo=max(0, xbest - 4*span); xhi=min(1, xbest+4*span)
          if xhi-xlo > 1e-10 and (0.1 < xbest < 0.9 or v < 1e-3):
             xr=np.linspace(xlo,xhi,40)
             wr=xr-v
             keep2=(1+wr)>1e-8
             if keep2.sum():
                num2=D[None,:]+wr[keep2][:,None]*infl[None,:]
                if 0<=2*i<L: num2[:,2*i]+=wr[keep2]**2
                np.maximum(num2,0,out=num2)
                val2=np.max(num2,axis=1)/((1+wr[keep2])**2)*2n
                j2=argmin; if val2[j2]<scbest: update
          if scbest < best_sc - 1e-12:
             best[i]=xbest
             ww=xbest-v
             D = D + ww*infl
             if 0<=2*i<L: D[2*i]+=ww*ww
             Snew=1+ww
             D = D/(Snew*Snew)
             # normalize a
             best = best.copy()? We can modify in place then scale
             best *= (1.0/Snew)
             # But best[i] already xbest unnormalized; after scaling becomes xbest/Snew. Good.
             best_sc=scbest
             improved=True
             # correct small negative D
             if D.min()<0: D=D.copy(); D[D<0]=0
       if not improved: break
    # final exact recompute
    sf=_score_norm(best)
    if sf<best_sc: best_sc=sf; best=best/sf? no, score changes? just update
    return best, best_sc
```
Need careful: We modify `best` in place and then `best *= 1/Snew`. But D update also. However `infl` was based on previous best. After scaling, D normalized. Good. But `best` may have all entries scaled, including i. For next coordinate, ok.
Potential issue: We use `best` in `infl` and then scale. While computing grid, `best` current. Good.
But if accept, `D` becomes normalized by Snew^2; `best` scaled. Score `best_sc=scbest` should equal `2n max(D)`. Good.
If due numerical D max differs, final recompute.

Caveat: `num = D[None,:] + ww[:,None]*infl[None,:]` memory. `def np.maximum(num,0,out=num)` works. But if num negative for some, okay.

If `v` = 1 and x=0, denom1, w=-1, num maybe? For i with v=1, other entries zero; D0=1; changing to 0 makes D all0 score? invalid sum0. But if v=1, norm sum1, score inf? Actually a single one: D=1, score2n large. Den for x=0: Snew=0 invalid; we filter 1+w>1e-8, so not accept. Good.

```
def _log_optimize(a_init, stop_time, alloc_s, seed=0, T_levels=None):
    if a_init is None: return None, inf
    a0=np.asarray(a_init,float).ravel().copy()
    if a0.size==0: return None, inf
    a0=np.clip...; s=a0.sum(); if s>0: a0/=s else ones
    if T_levels is None:
       T_levels = (1,4,16,64) if len(a0)<200 else (2,8,32,128)?
    opt=_LogLSE(a0,T=T_levels[0])
    y=opt.y0.copy()
    n=opt.n
    base_maxfun = int(max(500, min(8000, 3_000_000/max(1,n))))
    # level times proportions: total alloc = min(stop_time - now, alloc_s)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    total = max(10.0, min(alloc_s, stop_time - t0))
    fracs = [0.25,0.40,0.20,0.15] if len(T_levels)==4 else ...
    for idx,T in enumerate(T_levels):
       now=time.monotonic()

       if lt < 8: continue
       maxfun = int(total*base_maxfun/total? maybe base* (fracs?))
       y=_run_lsq(opt,y,T, now+lt, base_maxfun, 1e-9 if idx<2 else 1e-8)
       # If no exact improvement and time left, maybe continue
    # final sharp
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now < stop_time - 30:

       lt=min(stop_time-now, max(10,total*0.15))
```

`_run_lsq`:
```
def _run_lsq(opt, y, T, stop_time, maxfun, gtol=1e-8):
    if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 5: return y
    opt.T = float(T)
    deadline = stop_time - 8
    def fun(ya):
        return opt.objgrad(ya, deadline)
    def cb(x):
        return time.monotonic() > stop_time - 12
    bounds = [(-opt.bound, opt.bound)] * opt.y0.size
    try:
       res = minimize(fun, np.asarray(y,float), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                      options={'maxiter': max(10, maxfun//4), 'maxfun': maxfun, 'gtol': float(gtol), 'ftol': 1e-14, 'disp': False},
                      callback=cb)
       if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
           y = np.asarray(res.x,float)
    except _TimeUp:
       pass
    except Exception:
       pass
    # evaluate final
    try:
       a=opt._a(y); sc=_score_norm(a)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < opt.best_score - 1e-14:
           opt.best_score=sc; opt.best_a=a.copy(); opt.best_y=np.asarray(y,float).copy()
    except: pass
    return y
```

Detailed code for propose:

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    try: budget=float(budget_s)
    except: budget=1300.0
    budget=max(20.0,budget)
    hard=t0+budget-25.0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=_get_parent()
    best_a=parent.copy()
    best_sc=_score_norm(best_a)
    if not finite: best_a=np.ones(384); best_sc=_score_norm
    def consider(a): nonlocal best_a,best_sc ...
    # quick lengths
    hard_q = min(hard, t0 + max(30.0, budget*0.12))
    lengths=set()
    base=[32,48,64,96,128,160,192,256,320,384,448,512,576,640,768,896,1024,1280,1536,1792,2048]
    lengths.update(L for L in base if 32<=L<=2048)
    lengths.add(parent.size)
    if parent.size>0:
       for d in ( -64, -32, 64, 128, 256, 512):
          L=parent.size+d
          if 32<=L<=2048: lengths.add(L)
    # Remember quick scores
    quick=[]
    for L in sorted(lengths):
       if time.monotonic() > hard_q: break
       if L==parent.size:
          a=parent.copy()
       else:
          a=_interp(parent, L)
       sc=_score_norm(a)
       quick.append((sc,L))
       consider(a)
    quick.sort()
    # Primary length: best_a.size
    primary_len=best_a.size
    # primary stop
    primary_stop=min(hard-120, t0 + budget*0.55)
    if time.monotonic() < primary_stop - 15:
       alloc=min(primary_stop-time.monotonic(), max(60, budget*0.35))
       try: _log_optimize(best_a, primary_stop, alloc, seed=1000, n? )
       except: pass
       consider(best_a) # after _log_optimize? We need _log_optimize return.
```

```
if aopt is not None: consider(aopt)
```

Let's write `_log_optimize` with T levels parameter:
```
def _log_optimize(a_init, stop_time, alloc_s, seed=0, T_levels=None, maxfun_scale=1.0):
    a0=...
    n=a0.size
    if n<3: return a0, _score_norm(a0)
    if T_levels is None:
       if n<=128: T_levels=(2,8,32,128)
       elif n<=512: T_levels=(4,16,64,256)
       else: T_levels=(8,32,128,512)
    opt=_LogLSE(a0,T=T_levels[0])
    y=opt.y0.copy()
    base_maxfun=int(max(400, min(8000, 3_000_000/max(1,n) * maxfun_scale)))
    tstart=time.monotonic()
    total=max(5.0, min(float(alloc_s), max(0.0, stop_time-tstart)))
    fracs= np.linspace(0,1,len(T_levels)+1)
    for r,T in enumerate(T_levels):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= stop_time - 12: break
       # allow fraction of total but at least 8
       alloc_run = min(stop_time - now, max(8.0, total*(fracs[r+1]-fracs[r])))
       maxfun = int(max(200, base_maxfun*(fracs[r+1]-fracs[r])* (maxfun_scale)))
       y = _run_lsq(opt, y, T, now + alloc_run, maxfun, gtol=1e-9 if r<2 else 1e-8, maxiter maybe)
    # sharp final if time
    now=time.monotonic()
    if now < stop_time - 25:
       T_final = max(64, int(T_levels[-1]*1.5))
       alloc_run=min(stop_time-now, max(10,total*0.15))
       if alloc_run>15:
          y=_run_lsq(opt,y,T_final,now+alloc_run, base_maxfun//2, 1e-8)
    # restarts if alloc large
    if total > 60:
       rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+7)
       for r in range(2):
          now=time.monotonic()
          if now >= stop_time-25: break
          sigma=0.12*(r+1)
          y=opt.best_y + rng.normal(0,sigma,size=opt.y0.size)
          y=np.clip(y,-opt.bound,opt.bound)
          alloc_run=min(stop_time-now, max(8,total*0.08))
          if alloc_run>10:
             y=_run_lsq(opt,y,min(T_levels[1],64),now+alloc_run, base_maxfun//3,1e-8)
    # final best eval
    try:
       a=opt._a(y); sc=_score_norm(a); update opt
    except: pass
    return opt.best_a.copy(), float(opt.best_score)
```
But `base_maxfun*(frac)*` may be small; use not negative. If total small, base*frac maybe <200. max. For primary total 400, fracs: 0.25,0.35,0.25,0.15? Linspace 5: [0,0.2,0.4,0.6,0.8,1] if 4 levels -> fracs diff 0.2 each. base 8000 => 1600 each. Good. For total 30 quick, maxfun 600 each. Fine.

`fracs=np.linspace(0,1,len(T_levels)+1)` gives equal intervals. Good.

Let's draft precisely:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

class _TimeUp(Exception):
    pass

def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if h is None:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.size == 0 or float(p.sum()) < 1e-9:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    return p
```

Conv:
```
def _conv_self(a, N=None, L=None):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.zeros(0)
    if L is None: L=2*n-1
    if N is None: N=next_fast_len(max(1,L))
    if n==1:
       return np.array([float(a[0]*a[0])])
    F=rfft(a,N)
    c=irfft(F*F,N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
    return c
```
If n=1 and L=1, okay. For n=0, return.

`_score_norm`:
```
def _score_norm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return math.inf
    s=a.sum()
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300: return math.inf
    if abs(s-1.0) > 1e-12:
       a = a / s
    D=_conv_self(a)
    if D.size==0: return math.inf
    M=float(D.max())
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M < 0: return math.inf
    return 2.0 * a.size * M
```
If sum is nan, false? `math.isfinite(s)` handles. If a contains inf? `_score_norm` could produce nan. We can clean in consider. In log, a finite.

`_interp`:
```
def _interp_dense(a, n_new):
    a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n == n_new:
       out=a.copy()
    else:
       s=a.sum()
       if s<1e-300:
          out=np.ones(n_new)/n_new
       else:
          an=a/s
          x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
          x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
          f_old=an*n
          f_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, f_old)
          f_new=np.maximum(f_new,0.0)
          out=f_new/n_new
          ss=out.sum()
          if ss<1e-300:
             out=np.ones(n_new)/n_new
          else:
             out/=ss
    out=np.where(np.isfinite(out),out,0.0)
    out=np.clip(out,0.0,1000.0)
    out=out/out.sum() if out.sum()>0 else out
    return out
```
This normalizes.

LogLSE class. Need use `_conv_self` with N. For speed, define inside method:
```
F=rfft(a,self.N_conv); D=irfft(F*F,self.N_conv)[:self.L]; np.maximum(D,0.0,out=D)
```

```
y=np.full(n, -bound)
ratio=a/a[ref]
```

In `objgrad`, if `y` has wrong length? minimize will pass correct. For safety, if y.size != self.idx.size, reshape? Could return inf. But not needed.

```
def objgrad(self, y, deadline=None):
   if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
       raise _TimeUp
   y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
   if y.size != self.idx.size:
       # try pad/trim
       tmp=np.full(self.idx.size, 0.0)
       m=min(y.size,self.idx.size); tmp[:m]=y.reshape(-1)[:m]; y=tmp
   a=self._a(y)
   D=...
   Mmax=float(D.max()) if D.size else 0.0
   score=2.0*self.n*Mmax if math.isfinite(Mmax) else math.inf
   if math.isfinite(score) and score < self.best_score - 1e-14:
      self.best_score=score; self.best_a=a.copy(); self.best_y=y.copy()
   Dsafe=np.where(D > 1e-300, D, 1e-300)
   logD=np.log(Dsafe)
   m = float(logD.max())
   T=float(self.T)
   if T <= 1e-8:
       active = logD > m - 1e-10
       if not np.any(active): active=np.zeros(self.L,bool); active[0]=True
       alpha = np.zeros(self.L); alpha[active]=1.0/np.count_nonzero(active)
       H = float(m)
   else:
       ex = np.exp(np.maximum(T*(logD-m), -745.0))
       sume=ex.sum()
       if not math.isfinite(sume) or sume <= 0.0:
           return (1e300, np.zeros(self.idx.size))
       H = float(m + math.log(sume)/T)
       alpha = ex/sume
   s=np.zeros(self.L)
   np.divide(alpha, Dsafe, out=s, where=Dsafe > 0.0)
   C=self._corr(a,s)
   g=2.0*a*(C-1.0)
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
       return (1e300, np.zeros(self.idx.size))
   gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0
   if not math.isfinite(gmax) or gmax > 1e8:
       g = g * (1e8 / max(gmax,1e-12))
   return (H, g[self.idx].copy())
```
If T<=0, alpha based on active; H exact logmax. Good.

But active condition `logD > m - 1e-10`: if m -1e-10, active few. If no active due m -inf? Dsafe min finite. Good.

Now `_run_lsq`:
```
def _run_lsq(opt, y, T, stop_time, maxfun, gtol=1e-9):
   y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
   if time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 8 or opt.n <= 1:
       return y
   if len(y)==0: # one variable? no
       return y
   opt.T=float(T)
   deadline=stop_time-6
   def fun(yy):
       return opt.objgrad(yy, deadline)
   def cb(xx):
       return time.monotonic() > stop_time - 10
   bounds=[(-opt.bound, opt.bound) for _ in range(len(y))]
   maxiter=int(max(10, maxfun//3))
   try:
       res = minimize(fun, y, jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds,
                      options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': int(maxfun), 'gtol': float(gtol), 'ftol':1e-14, 'disp':False},
                      callback=cb)
       if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None:
           y=np.asarray(res.x,float)
   except _TimeUp:
       pass
   except Exception:
       pass
   try:
       a=opt._a(y); sc=_score_norm(a)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < opt.best_score - 1e-14:
           opt.best_score=sc; opt.best_a=a.copy(); opt.best_y=y.copy()
   except Exception:
       pass
   return y
```
Potential issue: `minimize` may not stop exactly at stop_time due line search; deadline in fun raises when > stop_time-6. If line search includes many fun calls after deadline, first raises. Good.

In `_log_optimize`, after `_run_lsq`, opt.best_y maybe old best. Good.

```
def _log_optimize(a_init, stop_time, alloc_s, seed=0, T_levels=None):
    a0=...
    n=a0.size
    if n<=1: return a0, _score_norm(a0)
    if T_levels is None:
      if n<=128: T_levels=(2,8,32,128)
      elif n<=512: T_levels=(4,16,64,256)
      else: T_levels=(8,32,128,512)

    y=opt.y0.copy()
    start=time.monotonic()
    total=max(5.0, min(float(alloc_s), max(0.0, stop_time-start)))
    base_maxfun=int(max(400, min(8000, 3500000.0/max(1,n))))
    fr=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,len(T_levels)+1)
    for r,T in enumerate(T_levels):
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now >= stop_time - 15: break
       frac=fr[r+1]-fr[r]
       run_stop=min(stop_time, now + min(stop_time-now, max(8.0, total*frac)))
       maxfun=int(max(200, base_maxfun*frac))*? 
       # total*frac could be small; base_maxfun*frac maybe okay. Let's just maxfun=int(max(200, base_maxfun*frac))
       if run_stop - now > 8:
           y=_run_lsq(opt,y,T,run_stop,maxfun,gtol=1e-9 if r<2 else 1e-8)

       if run_stop-now>15:

          sigma=0.12*(r+1)
          ytry=opt.best_y + rng.normal(0.0,sigma,size=opt.y0.size)

          if run_stop-now>12:
             y=_run_lsq(opt,ytry,min(T_levels[1],64.0),run_stop,max(200,base_maxfun//3),1e-8)
    # final eval current y
    try: ...
    return opt.best_a.copy(), float(opt.best_score)
```

```
def _vals(xvals):
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


class _TimeUp(Exception):
    pass


def _get_parent():
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if h is None:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        try:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if p.size == 0 or float(p.sum()) < 1e-9:
        p = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    return p


def _conv_self(a, N=None, L=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if L is None:
        L = 2 * n - 1
    if N is None:
        N = next_fast_len(max(1, L))
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    F = rfft(a, N)
    c = irfft(F * F, N)[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score_norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return math.inf
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        a = a / s
    D = _conv_self(a)
    if D.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    M = float(D.max())
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
        return math.inf
    return 2.0 * a.size * M


def _interp_dense(a, n_new):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == n_new:
        out = a.copy()
    else:
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 1e-300:
            out = np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n_new)
        else:
            an = a / s
            x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
            f_old = an * n
            f_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, f_old)
            f_new = np.maximum(f_new, 0.0)
            out = f_new / max(1, n_new)
            ss = float(out.sum())
            if ss < 1e-300:
                out = np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n_new)
            else:
                out = out / ss
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    ss = float(out.sum())
    if ss < 1e-300:
        out = np.ones(max(1, n_new), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n_new)
    else:
        out = out / ss
    return out


class _LogLSE:
    def __init__(self, a, T=40.0, bound=70.0):
        self.n = int(a.size)
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 1e-300:
            a = np.ones(self.n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, self.n)
            s = 1.0
        a = a / s
        self.bound = float(bound)
        self.T = float(T)
        self.ref = int(np.argmax(a))
        if a[self.ref] < 1e-300:
            a[self.ref] = 1e-300
            s = float(a.sum())
            a = a / s
        ratio = a / max(1e-300, a[self.ref])
        y = np.full(self.n, -self.bound, dtype=np.float64)
        pos = ratio > 0.0
        y[pos] = np.log(ratio[pos])
        y = np.clip(y, -self.bound, self.bound)
        ids = np.arange(self.n, dtype=int)
        self.idx = ids[ids != self.ref]
        self.y0 = y[self.idx].copy()
        self.L = 2 * self.n - 1
        self.N_conv = next_fast_len(max(1, self.L))
        self.N_corr = next_fast_len(max(1, 3 * self.n - 2))
        self.best_a = a.copy()
        self.best_score = _score_norm(self.best_a)
        self.best_y = self.y0.copy()

    def _a_from_y(self, y):
        n = self.n
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if y.size:
            if y.size < self.idx.size:
                tmp = np.zeros(self.idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
                tmp[:y.size] = y
                y = tmp
            else:
                y = y[:self.idx.size]
            z[self.idx] = y
        zmax = float(z.max())
        e = np.exp(z - zmax)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            e = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(n)
        return e / s

    def _corr_a_s(self, a, s):
        n = self.n
        L = self.L
        if n == 1:
            return np.array([float(a[0] * s[0])], dtype=np.float64)
        N = self.N_corr
        r = s[::-1]
        A = rfft(a, N)
        B = rfft(r, N)
        c = irfft(A * B, N)[:3 * n - 2]
        C = c[2 * n - 2 : n - 1 : -1]
        if C.size != n:
            C = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            C = np.ascontiguousarray(C)
        np.maximum(C, 0.0, out=C)
        return C

    def objgrad(self, y, deadline=None):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            raise _TimeUp()
        y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if y.size != self.idx.size:
            tmp = np.zeros(self.idx.size, dtype=np.float64)
            m = min(y.size, self.idx.size)
            if m:
                tmp[:m] = y[:m]
            y = tmp

        n = self.n
        a = self._a_from_y(y)

        F = rfft(a, self.N_conv)
        D = irfft(F * F, self.N_conv)[:self.L]
        if D.size != self.L:
            D = np.zeros(self.L, dtype=np.float64)
        np.maximum(D, 0.0, out=D)

        Mmax = float(D.max()) if D.size else 0.0
        if math.isfinite(Mmax):
            score = 2.0 * n * Mmax
            if score < self.best_score - 1e-14:
                self.best_score = float(score)
                self.best_a = a.copy()
                self.best_y = y.copy()
        else:
            score = math.inf

        Dsafe = np.where(D > 1e-300, D, 1e-300)
        logD = np.log(Dsafe)
        m = float(logD.max())
        T = float(self.T)

        if T <= 1e-8:
            active = logD > (m - 1e-10)
            if not np.any(active):
                active = np.zeros(self.L, dtype=bool)
                active[0] = True
            alpha = np.zeros(self.L, dtype=np.float64)
            alpha[active] = 1.0 / float(np.count_nonzero(active))
            H = float(m)
        else:
            tu = T * (logD - m)
            np.maximum(tu, -745.0, out=tu)
            ex = np.exp(tu)
            sume = float(ex.sum())
            if not math.isfinite(sume) or sume <= 0.0:
                return (1e300, np.zeros(self.idx.size, dtype=np.float64))
            H = float(m + math.log(sume) / T)
            alpha = ex / sume

        s = np.zeros(self.L, dtype=np.float64)
        np.divide(alpha, Dsafe, out=s, where=Dsafe > 0.0)

        C = self._corr_a_s(a, s)
        g = 2.0 * a * (C - 1.0)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            return (1e300, np.zeros(self.idx.size, dtype=np.float64))
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(gmax) or gmax > 1e8:
            g = g * (1e8 / max(gmax, 1e-12))
        return (H, g[self.idx].copy())


def _run_lsq(opt, y, T, stop_time, maxfun, gtol=1e-9):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if opt.n <= 1 or y.size == 0 or time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 8.0:
        return y

    opt.T = float(T)
    deadline = stop_time - 6.0

    def fun(yy):
        return opt.objgrad(yy, deadline)

    def cb(xx):
        return time.monotonic() > stop_time - 10.0

    bounds = [(-opt.bound, opt.bound) for _ in range(y.size)]
    maxiter = int(max(10, maxfun // 3))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun,
            y,
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                "maxiter": maxiter,
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "gtol": float(gtol),
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "disp": False,
            },
            callback=cb,
        )
        if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            y = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
    except _TimeUp:
        pass
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        a = opt._a_from_y(y)
        sc = _score_norm(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < opt.best_score - 1e-14:
            opt.best_score = float(sc)
            opt.best_a = a.copy()
            opt.best_y = y.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass
    return y


def _log_optimize(a_init, stop_time, seed=0, T_levels=None):
    a0 = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a0.size <= 1:
        if a0.size == 0:
            a0 = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64) / 32.0
        return a0, _score_norm(a0)

    if T_levels is None:
        if a0.size <= 128:
            T_levels = (2.0, 8.0, 32.0, 128.0)
        elif a0.size <= 512:
            T_levels = (4.0, 16.0, 64.0, 256.0)
        else:
            T_levels = (8.0, 32.0, 128.0, 512.0)

    opt = _LogLSE(a0, T=T_levels[0])
    y = opt.y0.copy()
    start = time.monotonic()
    total = max(5.0, min(float(stop_time - start), 1e9))
    base_maxfun = int(max(400, min(8000, 3_500_000.0 / max(1, a0.size))))
    fr = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(T_levels) + 1)

    for r, T in enumerate(T_levels):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= stop_time - 15.0:
            break
        frac = fr[r + 1] - fr[r]
        run_stop = min(stop_time, now + min(max(0.0, stop_time - now), max(8.0, total * frac)))
        if run_stop - now > 8.0:
            maxfun = int(max(250, base_maxfun * max(0.15, frac)))
            gtol = 1e-9 if r < 2 else 1e-8
            y = _run_lsq(opt, y, T, run_stop, maxfun, gtol)

    now = time.monotonic()
    if now < stop_time - 25.0 and total >= 30.0:
        Tfinal = max(80.0, float(T_levels[-1]) * 1.5)
        run_stop = min(stop_time, now + max(10.0, total * 0.15))
        if run_stop - now > 15.0:
            y = _run_lsq(opt, y, Tfinal, run_stop, max(300, base_maxfun // 2), 1e-8)

    if total >= 70.0:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 12345)
        for r in range(2):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= stop_time - 25.0:
                break
            sigma = 0.12 * (r + 1)
            ytry = opt.best_y + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=opt.y0.size)
            ytry = np.clip(ytry, -opt.bound, opt.bound)
            run_stop = min(stop_time, now + max(8.0, total * 0.07))
            if run_stop - now > 12.0:
                Tmid = min(float(T_levels[1]), 64.0)
                y = _run_lsq(opt, ytry, Tmid, run_stop, max(200, base_maxfun // 3), 1e-8)

    try:
        a = opt._a_from_y(y)
        sc = _score_norm(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < opt.best_score - 1e-14:
            opt.best_score = float(sc)
            opt.best_a = a.copy()
            opt.best_y = y.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass
    return opt.best_a.copy(), float(opt.best_score)


def _lp_polish(a, stop_time, max_rounds=4):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1:
        return a, _score_norm(a)
    if n > 512 or time.monotonic() >= stop_time - 20.0:
        return a, _score_norm(a)

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        a = a / s

    D = _conv_self(a)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = 2.0 * n * float(D.max())

    for _ in range(max_rounds):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= stop_time - 25.0:
            break
        M = float(_conv_self(best).max())
        if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        L = 2 * n - 1
        A = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for i in range(n):
            A[i:i + n, i] = best[i]

        try:
            res = linprog(
                -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64),
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=np.full(L, M, dtype=np.float64),
                bounds=[(0.0, 1.0)] * n,
                method="highs",
                options={
                    "presolve": True,
                    "time_limit": int(max(1, min(20, stop_time - now - 8))),
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            break
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs < 1e-12:
            break
        g = g / gs

        local_best = best.copy()
        local_best_sc = best_sc
        ts = np.concatenate([np.linspace(0.05, 0.75, 15), [0.9, 1.0]])
        for t in ts:
            t = float(t)
            if t <= 0.0:
                continue
            cand = (1.0 - t) * best + t * g
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
            cs = float(cand.sum())
            if cs < 1e-300:
                continue
            cand = cand / cs
            sc = _score_norm(cand)
            if sc < local_best_sc - 1e-13:
                local_best_sc = sc
                local_best = cand

        if local_best_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = local_best
            best_sc = local_best_sc
        else:
            break

    sc_final = _score_norm(best)
    if math.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
        best_sc = total
    return best, best_sc


def _coord_polish(a, stop_time, max_sweeps=2, seed=1234):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 1 or n > 768:
        return a, _score_norm(a)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    else:
        a = a / s

    best = a.copy()
    D = _conv_self(best)
    best_sc = float(2.0 * n * D.max())
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    L = 2 * n - 1

    for _sweep in range(max_sweeps):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= stop_time - 20.0:
            break
        D = _conv_self(best)
        best_sc = float(2.0 * n * D.max())
        improved = False
        order = rng.permutation(n)
        for i in order:
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= stop_time - 25.0:
                break
            v = float(best[i])
            twi = 2 * i
            infl = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            st = i
            en = min(i + n, L)
            mlen = en - st
            if mlen > 0:
                infl[st:en] = 2.0 * best[:mlen]

            base = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 64)
            if v > 1e-12:
                lo = max(0.0, 0.05 * v)
                hi = min(1.0, 12.0 * v + 1e-6)
                if hi > lo:
                    near = np.linspace(lo, hi, 24)
                else:
                    near = np.array([v], dtype=np.float64)
                extra = np.concatenate([near, [v, min(1.0, 2.0 * v), max(0.0, 0.5 * v)]])
            else:
                extra = np.geomspace(1e-8, 0.2, 24)

            xs = np.clip(np.unique(np.concatenate([base, extra])), 0.0, 1.0)
            if xs[0] != 0.0:
                xs = np.concatenate([[0.0], xs])
            if xs[-1] != 1.0:
                xs = np.concatenate([xs, [1.0]])
            if xs.size > 136:
                step = max(1, xs.size // 128)
                xs = xs[::step]
                if xs[0] != 0.0:
                    xs = np.concatenate([[0.0], xs])
                if xs[-1] != 1.0:
                    xs = np.concatenate([xs, [1.0]])

            def _vals(xvals):
                w = np.asarray(xvals, dtype=np.float64) - v
                keep = (1.0 + w) > 1e-9
                if not np.any(keep):
                    return (math.inf, 0.0)
                xx = xvals[keep]
                ww = w[keep]
                num = D[None, :] + ww[:, None] * infl[None, :]
                if 0 <= twi < L:
                    num[:, twi] += ww * ww
                np.maximum(num, 0.0, out=num)
                denom = (1.0 + ww) * (1.0 + ww)
                vals = np.max(num, axis=1) / denom * (2.0 * n)
                j = int(np.argmin(vals))
                return (float(vals[j]), float(xx[j]))

            val, xbest = _vals(xs)
            if np.isfinite(val) and xs.size > 3:
                span = (float(xs[-1]) - float(xs[0])) / max(1, xs.size - 1)
                xlo = max(0.0, xbest - 5.0 * span)
                xhi = min(1.0, xbest + 5.0 * span)
                if xhi - xlo > 1e-12:
                    xr = np.linspace(xlo, xhi, 36)
                    val2, xbest2 = _vals(xr)
                    if val2 < val:
                        val, xbest = val2, xbest2

            if val < best_sc - 1e-12:
                w = xbest - v
                D = D + w * infl
                if 0 <= twi < L:
                    D[twi] += w * w
                Snew = 1.0 + w
                if Snew > 1e-9:
                    D = D / (Snew * Snew)
                    best[i] = xbest
                    best *= (1.0 / Snew)
                    np.maximum(D, 0.0, out=D)
                    best_sc = float(val)
                    improved = True

        if not improved:
            break

    best = np.clip(best, 0.0, 1000.0)
    bs = float(best.sum())
    if bs < 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n, math.inf
    best = best / bs
    sc_final = _score_norm(best)
    if math.isfinite(sc_final):
        best_sc = min(best_sc, sc_final)
    return best, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    hard = t0 + budget - 25.0

    parent = _get_parent()
    best_a = parent.copy()
    best_sc = _score_norm(best_a)
    if not math.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64) / 384.0
        best_sc = _score_norm(best_a)

    def _consider(a):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            return
        sc = _score_norm(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()

    # Quick length screen by interpolating the parent density.
    max_len = 2048
    candidate_lengths = {32, 48, 64, 96, 128, 160, 192, 256, 320, 384,
                         448, 512, 576, 640, 768, 896, 1024, 1280, 1536, 1792, 2048}
    if parent.size:
        for d in (-96, -64, -32, 64, 128, 256, 512):
            L = parent.size + d
            if 32 <= L <= max_len:
                candidate_lengths.add(int(L))
        candidate_lengths.add(int(parent.size))
    lengths = sorted(candidate_lengths)

    q_stop = min(hard, t0 + max(20.0, 0.12 * budget))
    quick = []
    for L in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= q_stop - 5.0:
            break
        if L == parent.size:
            a = parent.copy()
        else:
            a = _interp_dense(parent, L)
        sc = _score_norm(a)
        if math.isfinite(sc):
            quick.append((sc, L))
        _consider(a)
    quick.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    # Main optimization on the current best length.
    primary_stop = min(hard - 120.0, max(time.monotonic() + 15.0, t0 + 0.55 * budget))
    if time.monotonic() < primary_stop - 15.0:
        try:
            aopt, sopt = _log_optimize(best_a.copy(), primary_stop, seed=10101)
            if aopt is not None:
                _consider(aopt)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Secondary lengths, chosen from the quick screen and preferably larger.
    secondary_stop = hard - 90.0
    if time.monotonic() < secondary_stop - 30.0 and best_sc > 1.5025:
        selected = []
        for _, L in quick[:6]:
            L = int(L)
            if L == best_a.size:
                continue
            if L not in selected:
                selected.append(L)
            if len(selected) >= 4:
                break
        larger = [L for L in lengths if L > best_a.size]
        if larger and larger[0] not in selected and len(selected) < 5:
            selected.insert(0, int(larger[0]))

        for idx, L in enumerate(selected):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= secondary_stop - 20.0:
                break
            if best_sc < 1.50275:
                break
            start_stop = min(secondary_stop, now + max(30.0, min(180.0, 0.35 * (secondary_stop - now))))
            init = _interp_dense(best_a, L)
            try:
                Tmid = (8.0, 32.0, 128.0, 512.0) if L <= 512 else (16.0, 64.0, 256.0, 1024.0)
                aopt, sopt = _log_optimize(init, start_stop, seed=50000 + L, T_levels=Tmid)
                if aopt is not None:
                    _consider(aopt)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # A few shape restarts if still far from target.
    if time.monotonic() < secondary_stop - 40.0 and best_sc > 1.5035:
        ns = int(best_a.size)
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, ns)
        shapes = [
            1.0 + 3.0 * x,
            1.0 + 3.0 * (1.0 - x),
            np.maximum(0.05, 1.0 - 2.0 * x),
            np.maximum(0.05, 2.0 * x - 1.0),
        ]
        for k, sh in enumerate(shapes):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now >= secondary_stop - 25.0:
                break
            a = np.asarray(sh, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(a.sum())
            if s > 0:
                a = a / s
            else:
                continue
            _consider(a)
            stop = min(secondary_stop, now + max(20.0, min(70.0, 0.25 * (secondary_stop - now))))
            try:
                Tshape = (8.0, 40.0, 160.0) if ns <= 512 else (16.0, 80.0, 320.0)
                aopt, sopt = _log_optimize(a, stop, seed=70000 + k, T_levels=Tshape)
                if aopt is not None:
                    _consider(aopt)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final polish: LP, higher-temperature local search, coordinate descent.
    if time.monotonic() < hard - 70.0:
        if best_a.size <= 512:
            lp_stop = min(hard - 45.0, time.monotonic() + min(45.0, 0.25 * max(0.0, hard - time.monotonic())))
            try:
                alp, scp = _lp_polish(best_a.copy(), lp_stop, max_rounds=3)
                if alp is not None:
                    _consider(alp)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.monotonic() < hard - 35.0:
        log_stop = min(hard - 18.0, time.monotonic() + min(160.0, 0.65 * max(0.0, hard - time.monotonic())))
        if log_stop > time.monotonic() + 15.0:
            nfin = int(best_a.size)
            Tfin = (16.0, 64.0, 256.0, 1024.0) if nfin <= 512 else (32.0, 128.0, 512.0, 2048.0)
            try:
                aopt, sopt = _log_optimize(best_a.copy(), log_stop, seed=99991, T_levels=Tfin)
                if aopt is not None:
                    _consider(aopt)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if time.monotonic() < hard - 12.0 and best_a.size <= 768:
        coord_stop = hard - 4.0
        try:
            apc, scp = _coord_polish(best_a.copy(), coord_stop, max_sweeps=2, seed=31337)
            if apc is not None:
                _consider(apc)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Final validation using the authoritative evaluator.
    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if best_a.size == 0 or float(best_a.sum()) < 0.01:
        best_a = parent.copy()
        best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
        best_a = np.clip(best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if best_a.size == 0 or float(best_a.sum()) < 0.01:
        return [1.0]

    try:
        final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a]))
    except Exception:
        final_sc = math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(final_sc):
        try:
            final_sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in parent]))
        except Exception:
            final_sc = math.inf
        if math.isfinite(final_sc):
            return [float(x) for x in parent]
        return [1.0]

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
